   Start with a simple drawing exercise to learn canvas settings, the role of each layer, brush adjustments and PNG or native-project export in Aquarell.

3. ### [Aquarell brush basics: compare size and opacity in a nine-cell study](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-brush-size-opacity/>)

   Compare brush size and opacity in a nine-cell exercise, distinguishing a single stroke, repeated colour buildup and layer opacity.

   Getting started 9 min read
4. ### [Aquarell brush spacing: continuous lines, dotted textures and borders](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-brush-spacing/>)

   Keep brush size fixed and compare low, medium and high spacing, then use the results for dotted decoration and hand-drawn borders.

   Getting started 9 min read
5. ### [Aquarell layers: separate sketch, colour and details](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-three-layer-workflow/>)

   Build a three-layer workflow, understand layer order, the active layer and visibility, then use a simple plant illustration to check that edits stay independent.

   Getting started 9 min read
6. ### [Aquarell layer opacity: lighten sketches and adjust overlapping colour](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-layer-opacity/>)

   Use sketches, shadows and highlights to understand what layer opacity affects, and distinguish brush transparency from compositing a whole layer.

   Getting started 9 min read
7. ### [Aquarell colour basics: build a five-colour illustration palette](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-limited-palette/>)

8. ### [Aquarell colour sampling: keep touch-ups, shadows and details consistent](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-canvas-color-picking/>)

   Sample from existing colour areas rather than repeatedly guessing, and check deviations caused by transparent overlaps and edge sampling.

   Getting started 9 min read
9. ### [Draw a leaf in Aquarell: from outline to soft veins](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/aquarell-paint-leaf/>)

   Draw a simple leaf in three layers, practising shape, colour areas, one-sided shadow and a hierarchy of veins, then export your exercise.

## ManYue 260 tutorials

[Follow the learning path](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/manyue/>)

1. ### [ManYue getting started: import local comics and read offline](<https://allluckly.cn/en/guides/manyue-getting-started/>)

   Getting started 6 min read

   Getting started 6 min read

   Getting started 6 min read

   Sync and troubleshooting 6 min read
